## [4.3.6]
> 20 Apr 2026

### Added

- Add a placeholder as user feedback making clear how much background pages are generated and their generation progress ([dddc8ba](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/dddc8bad650936d263d3cb0bdde255964fead7da))
- Add permanent report generation banner across whole application and also add an abort report generation button ([22144e8](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/22144e871aa98532f003cc26582b3ffc4cb9e023))
- Add progress and abort functions to permanent report banner and also show report generation status ([795511b](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/795511bcc8fdfee650d41089f6454e53672083ea))
- Add new reachability workflow to overcome starneg leaflet screenshot issue with active isochrones. now first generate poi dataset für spatial unit (including screenshot process without errors) and then let users optionally add isochrones or indicator labels ([d85af2e](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/d85af2ecb9584b5404dd39d21405f436dc34df61))

### Changed

- Cherry pick spatial filter related commits into version/4 branch (display improvements and bugfixes) ([7212dde](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/7212ddebc66aba72c5713e1e8dbb2678e8403cde))
- Improve reporting perfmroance by only showing some spatial unit features in preview and generate the rest in the background ([ab8f1ad](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/ab8f1add7ee5e5d2091a7ae19d16983185f6eb63))
- Takeover generated pages from reportingIndicatorAdd menu to reportingOverview menu, improving performance, user only has to wait once for the pages to generate ([0837acc](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/0837acc625a95f801a16d2cdbae26cd04493ee97))
- Boost preview dataTable reporting page generation to allow full reporting overview and afterwards generate alle other singleFeature pages asynchronously ([765f12e](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/765f12ecb6852ebe66b307537922b5bcce8e2883))
- Reporting: new user workflow: configure first then activate report generation to make sure User has full control over generated content ([9b7783a](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/9b7783a774c69995cdf9041711bb2312a05dd9fd))
- Only show preview area in reporting if report is actually generated to let users focus on configuration in the beginning ([7978637](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/7978637fc2095e76032cb1beef7b0e59892d48a0))
- Give leafllet more time prior to generating basemap screenshots to improve image quality and placement ([d8f232b](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/d8f232b1a615fba5783d1dee02ccf185120a7e66))
- Adjust selection of spatial units in reporting ([e334afc](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/e334afcf2146fb4ada9c7d24edc9702b9b65e6b9))
- Ensure that on import of report configuration file, each page is fully checked for map screenshots etc. ([6169b0b](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/6169b0b323fab4fbd6c638ca3a9ce80028dd7a60))
- More robust echarts map exports in reporting ([fa0a251](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/fa0a2516e32bab17bda0cb9a4c68472800f4042f))
- Enforce only single oriented pages due to new workflow and preview mechanics in reporting ([981c10c](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/981c10c4a6814ca269fddc98fea16bbaa0076658))
- Adjust background page generation comment ([f97c77f](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/f97c77f801ca61a2e0257dd5a21cabcab5c15ab6))
- If many datatable pages are required than also hide the most of them in preview and only generate them in the background. ([a713480](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/a71348044cf06c0318b4e329e35d13d0bb80f252))
- Improve reporting banner layout and add a button to go back to reporting menu if users navigate other parts of KomMonitor ([d6d9bdd](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/d6d9bddb93da4aa543ac8b59e8c485acb7d59110))
- Make leaflet screenshot process more robust due to more timeouts and focus only first echarts series when taking the leaflet screenshot ([7b8fb6e](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/7b8fb6e0badb2fe20d7550d4ac2cb031ace151c7))
- Switch tab positions in reachability reports. tab 1 POI layer; tab 2 spatial unit; tab 3 & 4 isochrones and indicator (activated after basic report generation completed) ([8122c3c](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/8122c3c02396cb29201fbac0482633aa89779aed))
- Move background reporting processor section into index.html to ensure leaflet screenshots are taken correctly from remaining background pages on closed reporting modal ([24c0a03](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/24c0a034e82bed4b304e54d6e1eee6a0aedfd749))

- Store leaflet screenshots in indexedDB including template name, as map containers may have different sizes in different templates ([7f5a619](https://github.com/KomMonitor/web-client/commit/7f5a6194da68145a3635245cae9666d394db4890))
